### Audit of what no test had touched (2026-11-05, night)

Not the same tests again: what no session had ever done.

- **Mods players combine with this one, never loaded.** SpeedX (patches `Cmd.Wait`), ModConfig and "Unlimited: No Player Limit" (transpiles every `Serialize`; `RelicStateReuse` and the upgrade bits allow its transpiler by fingerprint) were on disk but not in the account's mod list: no test had run with them. `BDTEST_EXTRA_MODS=SpeedX,ModConfig,sts2unlimited` (new, `parlib.ps1`) adds them to a copy's test mods. With them, on public and beta: `ab-2k` 54, `outside-2k` 48, `autoslay-2k` 613 / 387, `chemical-x-big` 23 lines identical mod off and on; `effects-check`, `audit-toggles-2k`, `fast-start-check`, `snapcheck-hooks`, `relics-verify-0` no problem; `coop`, `coopfast`, `coopclone`, `audit-cooptoggle`, `audit-coopdeath`, `snapcheckcoop`, `coop4`: peers agree. No back-off reported from them.
- **Every game language** (`audit-languages`): card texts formatted with `TextPoolReuse` on and off, deck view sort orders (culture sort keys), tabs and node recycling, each against the game's own, in the 14 languages: all identical, on both versions.
- **A player dying in co-op** (`audit-coopdeath`, the client dies on its second turn, the host plays on to round 7): peers agree on every enemy state, both versions.
- **Turned off at the start** (`started-off.ps1`, new: the saved setting off in a test slot): no patch applied, `bdopt` says inactive, a fight plays, turning it on says "at the next start"; both versions.
- **Load order**: covered by design: every patch applies over the frames after all mods have loaded (`ApplyOverFrames`), so Limitless and BaseLib are always there already (`baselib-late`: no Limitless patch skipped then reapplied).
- **Threads**: the game runs no logic off the main thread (no `Task.Run` or thread outside Steam, analytics and logging); the patches that check it are where saves or Godot callbacks could come from another thread.
- **Debug and Release**: no test plays a combat with the Release build (the test runner isn't in it). Every `#if BENCH` and `#else` branch read: only counters, checks and test switches; the Release build applies all 37 patches in game.

**Release checks v1.0.0 (2026-11-06).** The V1 goal accepted by the user on Step V's numbers. The code is the one of the batteries above but the version (`check_versions`, installed and published: the verified ones): the full battery of "Last audit before V1" (both copies, no baselines, every sweep, co-op with and without other mods, four peers, settings, leaks, mutations 38/38, 8,400-card A/Bs) and the checks of "Audit of what no test had touched" (SpeedX, ModConfig and Unlimited loaded, every language, a dead co-op player, started off). With the version changed: `settings.ps1` ok (the menu shows `v1.0.0` at the top right), `coop-required.ps1` refused ("BigDeckOptimization-v1.0.0"); Release package in game on both copies: `Initialized v1.0.0`, 37 patches applied, none skipped, failed or disabled, HookSkip 147 / 148, 0 left vanilla; `tools/compat` 0 problem on both. The "early development" disclaimer is gone from the Workshop page and the README (the user, after two weeks on the Workshop with no bug report since the beta one). Not run: a co-op game between people on separate PCs, `bdopt` typed by hand in the Release build, normal game speed.

### Step V (2026-11-05): what the rapid plays' 1% low really measures

The last phase under 120 fps at 10,000 cards in co-op. Rendered, public, 189 relics per player, Apotheosis and Cascade avoided, `ladder.ps1 -PlaysOnly -Turns 10`. `-ReleaseLike` (new): the sampled checks at a Release build's rates, what Debug does 16 times as often (`DeckCountMemo`, `RelicStateReuse.Scalar`); the test tooling can't leave a session, so this is as close to Release as a scenario gets. 0 divergence in every session.

| Rapid plays | sessions, frames, plays | 1% low median | frames over 8 ms | per card played | frame p99 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Co-op, starting deck, no relic, Debug (`lad-fl*c0`) | 3, 3,665 | 147 | 0.55% | | 6.8 ms |
| Co-op, 935 cards, Debug (`lad-fl*c1`) | 3, 5,898 | 100 | 3.02% | | 9.9 ms |
| Co-op, 11,230 cards, Debug (`lad-fl*c11`) | 3, 4,955, 470 | 92 | 3.69% | 0.39 | 10.9 ms |
| Co-op, 11,230 cards, release-like (`lad-rl*c11`) | 3, 4,888, 469 | 103 | 3.21% | 0.33 | 9.8 ms |
| Solo, 11,230 cards, release-like (`lad-rs*s11`) | 2, 3,304, 158 | 120 | 1.54% | 0.32 | 8.4 ms |
| Co-op, 11,230 cards, no relic, release-like (`lad-n*c11`) | 3, 4,133, 445 | 116 | 1.60% | 0.15 | 8.6 ms |
| Co-op, both peers rendered, the host (`lad-br*c11`) | 2, 3,291, 313 | 96 | 3.49% | 0.37 | 10.5 ms |

(Without relics players die on turn 4: `BUFFER_POWER` on them in `lad-n*`. A first pair without it, 871 frames, had suggested relics weigh little: wrong. The rendered client of `lad-br*` runs at 60 fps, a hidden window's cap: its worst frame is 23 ms, nothing else to read about 120 fps.)

- **Deck size no longer matters here**: twelve times the cards costs 8 fps of 1% low.
- **Co-op adds nothing per card**: 0.33 frames over 8 ms per card played, 0.32 solo. Both peers play at once in this test: it is the density of plays, ~24 cards a second in those windows.
- **189 relics double what a card costs** (0.15 -> 0.33), the largest factor found.
- **Debug understated** this phase by ~10 fps: the numbers of earlier steps are low by about that.
- **These frames are the engine's.** `-Probe` (`lad-pr1c11`), 213 frames of 8 ms or more, median 10 ms: 16% of their time is in the probed C# at top level, 118 of them hold under 1 ms of it, GC is in one. Without the checksum altogether, 56 of 213 would fall under 8.33 ms; without everything named, 89.

**Native traces with and without relics** (`lad-etw6c11`, `lad-etw7c11`, release-like, 8 turns, 1 kHz; `tools/profiling/wpr-watch.ps1` runs WPR from an administrator window on flags the test session writes; `etwtimeline.py` finds the windows of plays in the trace itself, as the game's log has no wall clock and reaches the disk late). Same plays, 15.3 against 12.2 s, 4,053 and 3,964 frames, 91 against 53 of 8 ms or more: relics add ~0.7 ms to every frame, and the split between process and draw doesn't move (53 / 44%). Of the 2,931 samples they add: `RenderingServerDefault::_draw` 1,292 (`render_canvas` 644, `swap_buffers` 523), redraws 623 (`RichTextLabel` 398), C# continuations ~460, tweens 245. `_cull_canvas_item` only 159: what isn't on screen is already culled. No part stands out: more nodes to draw and to animate, as Step S concluded for the relic bar. Nothing to cut without changing what is shown.

So this phase's number is one frame of ~10 ms for every 2 or 3 cards, at a rate no player reaches. At a player's pace (`mouse plays`, ~0.7 cards a second, 1% low 210-251 at 11,230 cards, `lad-finc11`, v0.12.0) those frames are well under 1% of all frames. Going under 8.3 ms means less work in the engine: fewer effects on screen, a visual choice this mod hasn't made. No lead in C#.

### Harmony rebuilds that dropped another mod's patches (2026-11-10, v1.0.1)

Reported from a friend's co-op game with v1.0.0 (Limitless, BaseLib, BetterSpire2, CampfireTrade, LobbyBanlist): after opening a player's profile, the back button didn't bring the map back. Their log: `InvalidCastException: NGlobalUi to ScrollContainer` in Limitless's `ShowHidePatches.AnimShow/AnimHidePostfix`, at every open and close, after `InlineGuard: NGlobalUi._Ready left as compiled: Common Language Runtime detected an invalid program.`

- Limitless moves the relic bar into a scroll container from a node BaseLib adds in a postfix on `NGlobalUi._Ready` (`IAddedNodes<T>.UnconditionalAdd`). That postfix is declared in a generic interface. Harmony stores patches serialized and, at each rebuild, refinds a patch method by module and token (`AccessTools.GetMethodByModuleAndToken`): for a method of a generic type that is the open generic definition, which the JIT refuses. Only the first build, made from the method BaseLib passed, works: every later rebuild of that method fails, whoever asks for it. Same for `NRewardButton._Ready` (in every log of this machine) and BaseLib's `ExtendedSavePatches+GenericPostfix<,>` on the six `MegaCritSerializerContext.Serializable*PropInit` (found with `BDTEST_REBUILD_ALL=1`).
- Harmony 2.4.2 `PatchTools.DetourMethod` disposes the method's detour, then creates the new one; the JIT failure comes in between. The method was left with no detour: BaseLib's postfix gone, so the relic bar stayed under `NGlobalUi`, and Limitless's postfixes threw on its first show or hide. The 2026-11-04 audit had read the log line as harmless.
- Why only their game: `InlineGuard` rebuilt `NGlobalUi._Ready` there (138 to 144 methods compiled again), not here (115 to 118): it reached a protected callee there. `NRewardButton._Ready` lost BaseLib's added nodes here too, unseen.
- Any rebuild by this mod could do it, not only `InlineGuard`'s: every Patch and Unpatch (`PatchGuard.Patch`, `Watch`, `Remove`) rebuilds from every mod's patches. Hence `HarmonyRebuild` (safety nets above): refusal before touching anything, restoring after a failure, a check of every patched method once the patches are in.

Proof (public copy, Debug, `par.ps1`, the account's mods): `rebuild-check` passes, `NGlobalUi._Ready` and `NRewardButton._Ready` refused and kept, a forced invalid rebuild of `NRelicInventory.AnimShow` restored (`restored 1`), 860 patched methods detoured, in a run the relic bar in its `ScrollContainer` and AnimShow/AnimHide without exception; with `BDTEST_REBUILD_ALL=1` 695 methods compiled again, 8 refused, 0 lost, the same checks pass. The control (`BDTEST_REBUILD_RAW=1`, v1.0.0's way, and `rebuild-control`) reproduces the report: `PATCHES LOST`, relic bar parent `NGlobalUi`, `AnimHide threw InvalidCastException: Unable to cast object of type 'NGlobalUi' to type 'ScrollContainer'`. The same patches apply as in v1.0.0, none refused.

Regression (Debug, no baselines): public `ab-2k` 54, `outside-2k` 48, `pile-cards-2k` 131, `combats3-2k` 63, `shuffle` 55 lines identical, `audit-toggles-2k`, `audit-conflict-ab`, `audit-listeners-ab` identical to `ab-2k-off`, `audit-deckcount-fault` to `deckcount-off`; `effects-check` 53/56 (the usual three); `release-checks`, `snapcheck-hooks`, `clonememo-check` (4,741 verified), `audit-finished-room-on`, `audit-verifier-relics`, `audit-gc-latency` as expected; co-op `coopfast`, `audit-coopdeath`, `audit-cooptoggle`, `outside-coop`, `outside-coopoff`, `coopclone` peers agree, `audit-waitspin` 2,001; with SpeedX, ModConfig and Unlimited `ab-2k` identical, 1,160 patched methods detoured; mutations 38/38; `started-off`, `settings.ps1`, `baselib-optional.ps1`, `limitless-switches.ps1` no problem. With every patched method rebuilt (`BDTEST_REBUILD_ALL=1`): `ab-2k-on` identical to `ab-2k-off`, `effects-check` 53/56: this mod's own patches survive any rebuild. Beta v0.111: `ab-2k`, `outside-2k`, `audit-toggles-2k`, `audit-conflict-ab` identical, `effects-check` 53/56, 869 methods detoured, `coopfast` and `audit-coopdeath` agree. Release DLL: `tools/compat` 0 problem on both.

**Release checks v1.0.1 (2026-11-10).** On top of the above: `check_versions` all verified ones; Release package in game on both copies (`release-incopy.ps1`): `Initialized v1.0.1`, 37 patches applied, none skipped, failed or disabled, `HarmonyRebuild: all 848 patched methods detoured` (1 refused, 0 restored, 0 lost); sweeps without baselines: relics 4,455 / 4,485, potions 328 / 333, encounters 3,146 / 3,321, cards 3,452 / 3,555, powers 5,240 / 5,342 lines identical (public / beta, the v0.12.0 counts; items stopping a session do so mod off too); public: `coop4` four peers agree, `coop` and `coopleak` agree (`coopleak` heap 245.8 -> 248.8 MB over fights 21 to 26, run cards +24 a fight, the rewards), `fast-start-check` (15 waits skipped, none with the option off or the mod off), `pile-cards-off/on` at 8,400 cards 131 lines identical. Not run this time: `memory-on`, `memory-runs-on/off` (no code a run keeps changed), `coop-required.ps1` (manifest unchanged), `menu-languages`, a real co-op game between separate PCs.

StS2 Clone Optimizer (Workshop 3747573917, v0.1.0, `affects_gameplay: false`): one prefix on `CloneRestSiteOption.OnSelect` (Limitless patches it too), which above 50 Clone cards clones in chunks over frames instead of one by one. Nothing of this mod there. `cloneopt-off` vs `cloneopt-on` (`BDTEST_EXTRA_MODS=Sts2CloneOptimizer`, 1,880 -> 9,480 cards) identical on both versions and identical to `clone-off` without it (its result is the game's: a player without it can't desync there); `coopclone` with it, peers agree.

Audit of the same kind over every patch: this mod's only Harmony calls are in `PatchGuard` and `InlineGuard` (all through `HarmonyRebuild` now); no patch method of this mod is declared in a generic type (other mods' rebuilds keep it); the transpilers depend only on their input IL (the static flags some set are read only right after their Apply); every prefix that skips the original backs off when another mod patches it, except `FastCombatStart`'s waits (on purpose, see "The audit's other leads"); node casts on other mods' structures: `EnchantmentTabReuse` now checks Limitless's tab template before using it (another shape renders through Limitless instead of faulting the patch).

### BaseLib 3.4.8 (2026-11-13)

Published 2026-11-12. `tools/fpdiff review` against the GitHub release DLL of 3.4.7: 289 fingerprints the same, two changed. The Harmony targets of both versions compared too (decompiled sources): new patches on `Hook.ModifyBlock`, `BlockVar`/`CalculatedBlockVar.UpdateCardPreview`, a second on `PotionModel.HoverTips`, the `NetHostGameService` constructor, and per-version tween patches for the beta's `CardPileCmd.GetTweenForCardsChangingPiles`; none on code this mod mirrors except the first.

- `ModifyHealAmountPatches.ModifyAdditive` / `ModifyMultiplicative` (HookSkip's BaseLib hooks) were left vanilla: the combat state parameter is now `ICombatState` instead of `object`, and the walk calls `runState.IterateHookListeners(combatState)` directly instead of through `BetaMainCompatibility`'s reflection. Still one RunState walk, the one the prefix filters: equivalents in `EquivalentFingerprints.cs`.
- `Hook.ModifyBlock` was left vanilla (`also patched by BaseLib`): BaseLib makes `CardModifier.ModifyBaseBlockAdditive/Multiplicative` work, through a prefix on `block` and a transpiler inserting `num = ModifyBaseBlockMultiplicative(num, props, cardSource)` before the first `ldarg combatState`, the start of the first walk. No walk added, the result independent of the listeners walked, compatible with HookSkip's redirect in either order: both added to HookSkip's allowed patches (now checked on a hook's own method too, not only its state machine), with the two callees fingerprinted (they loop over `cardSource.GetModifiers()`).
- The rest unchanged for this mod: `AfterCardPlayedPatch.AfterPlay` (the transpiler) is the same, only the method it injects copies the modifier list (`ToList()`); `HookUtils` now swallows a listener's exception (BaseLib's own hooks, not filtered here); `CardModifier` saves drop unknown modifiers; the menu's `NModConfigSubmenu.GetModTitle` handles a prefix without a trailing dash.

Proof (public copy, Debug, Limitless 1.2.1 installed meanwhile): `tools/compat` 0 problem; `release-checks` 0 part left vanilla (3 before the change, the `ModifyBlock` back-off gone in the same session); `ab-2k` 54, `pile-cards-2k` 131 lines identical without baselines; `effects-check` 53/56 (the usual three); `turns-32k-on` 31,800 cards, 0 left vanilla, verify mismatches 0; the back-offs (the check changed): `audit-conflict-ab` 54 lines identical to `ab-2k-off`, mutations 38/38. Not run: the beta copy, co-op.

### Game beta v0.113.0 (2026-11-13)

`public-beta` moved from v0.111.0 to v0.113.0 (build 26625880, 2026-11-12). Downloaded with SteamCMD into `C:\Games\StS2-beta-0.113.0` (Steam stays on public), `refs/v0.113.0`, decompiled in `decompiled/sts2-beta-0.113`. v1.0.1 on it: no crash, the changed parts vanilla, but FastListeners (`MultiplayerScalingModel` gone: its mirrors don't load) and so HookSkip off. `tools/fpdiff review` (v0.107.1 constants): 271 the same, 13 changed, 2 signatures.

- **The walks** (FastListeners): v0.113 adds the run models then the dominion at the end of both walks (the multiplayer scaling model is now one of the run models), and `Contains` accepts `RunModel` and `DominionModel` instead of `MultiplayerScalingModel` (abstract types deriving straight from `AbstractModel`, disjoint from the others it tests). The end of each walk and the accepted types are chosen at load from the fingerprints of both versions; the v0.107 end is a `[PatchPart("scaling")]` (another version's members, checked on its own, never JIT-compiled on v0.113), the v0.113 one reads the new properties through compiled getters. ListenerCache invalidates on the dominion's setter (its only writer: checked).
- **The damage hook** (HookFilter): v0.113's `ModifyDamageInternal` has a third loop, `ModifyDamageMultiplicativeLate` (default ×1, same code as the multiplicative one), after the multiplicative one in the same flag block: mirrored (`PrefixWithLate`, chosen by fingerprint), its listeners filtered as the others (hook 9, absent before v0.113).
- **HookSkip**: `classify.py` with the three versions: v0.113 150 hooks (the new arrow hooks among them), 4 unsafe (AfterTakingExtraTurn, TryReplaceDefaultRoomRewards, ModifyUnblockedDamageTarget, ModifyPreBlockDamageTarget: new code shapes), helpers as v0.111. The v0.107.1 and v0.111.0 rows unchanged.
- **Equivalent** (`EquivalentFingerprints.cs`, each entry says why): `CardPile.AddInternal` (a Shoot card may give an arrow slot: no model, the Fool's UI only), `Hook.IterateCombatHookListeners` (`ShouldSkipCombatEffect` is the expression it replaced), `NCardFlyShuffleVfx.PlayAnim` (await type only), `NHoverTipSet.Init` (right-to-left layout), `CheckMouseDragThreshold` (squared distances).
- **Test tooling**: `NGame.StartNewSingleplayerRun` takes a player, the card selectors moved to per-player stacks of the run with one static test selector (`AutoTest.SelectorStack` stacks this tooling's selectors on it, `tomenu` disposes them), `AutoSlayer.Start` takes a mode, shop purchases go through `RunManager.MerchantSynchronizer` (`Bench/AutoTestCompat.cs`, reflection, every version); the shop waits for `NMerchantRoom.Instance` (the node path changed), and AutoSlay's `WaitHelper.ForTask` (v0.113's room-entered wait, 30 s) gets the longer test limits like its other waits. `tools/compat` reports `[PatchPart]` methods apart.
- ControllerActionSkip runs but has nothing to skip on v0.113: the game maps the old controller actions again.

### Audit of 2026-11-15: every line, eight auditors

Eight read-only audits, each reading every line of its part and the game and mod code it mirrors, skips or relies on in v0.107.1, v0.111.0 and v0.113.1 (listener walks; checksum snapshots; hook filtering and `classify.py`; the Limitless patches against a fresh 1.2.1 decompile; piles, card ids and relic state; combat flow and UI; infrastructure and settings; the test tooling's proofs). Each finding checked against the code before a change; the same agents then fixed their own files. No crash, desync or state difference with today's game and mods; what was found are holes a game update or another mod could open, a few real small bugs, and test tooling that could pass while wrong. `decompiled/mods/Limitless` was still 1.1.7 (the 1.2.1 review had decompiled it elsewhere): now 1.2.1, 1.1.7 kept in `decompiled/mods/Limitless-1.1.7`.

Real today (small):

- **InlineGuard** cached "doesn't reach a protected method" by method only: a method first searched as a depth-0 callee was never searched deeper as a patched method, and a cycle's recursion guard stayed as a final `false`. A patched method reaching a write-tracked setter through a small callee could keep the inlined setter. Now cached by depth searched, no recursion guard (the depth ends cycles).
- **HoverTipReuse** reused a panel when the tips were the same, though the game places it again from the hitbox at each rebuild: a creature moved or resized with the same tips (Osty growing, enemies laid out again for a summon) kept the panel at the old place. Now also compares the hitbox's global position, size and scale and the viewport size; backs off on patches to every `NHoverTipSet` method a reuse skips (v0.113's `CreateAndRegister`, `ReplaceExistingTip`, `ConnectTreeExiting`, `SetAlignment(Rect2)` among them), each pinned per version.
- **FastListeners' recorded moves** (up to 4,096, holding piles and cards) kept the left run alive until the next run's first shuffle: released at `RunManager.CleanUp`.
- **Off at start for good**: `SavedOff` read the cfg's `Enabled: False` whenever BaseLib was listed, but only the BaseLib menu can save a change; with the menu failing (BaseLib broken by an update, an older BaseLib), the mod stayed off at every start and `bdopt on` saved nothing. Now off at start only when the menu registered and loaded "off"; a menu failing after loading "off" turns the master switch back on.

Back-offs and fallbacks (a game update or another mod):

- **SnapshotCardReuse**: card and pile bits stand for the whole serializer chain, fingerprinted (`CardState`, `SerializableCard`, `SerializableEnchantment`, `SavedProperties` and `WritePropertyName` (two versions), `CombatPileState`, `WriteInt`, BaseLib's two `WriteExtended` and `ExtendedSaveHandlers.Write`); another mod's patch there or on the closed `WriteList<CardState>`/`WriteList<CombatPileState>` leaves bits to the game (only BaseLib's known postfixes and Unlimited's known transpiler allowed; before, only `CardState.Serialize`'s prefixes and postfixes were watched). Bits carry an epoch (id cache hash and widths, BaseLib saves, `CanModifyGameplay`); none kept for objects saving an `int[]` (changed in place). Trust also requires the private getters handing out `_keywords` and the MultiEnchantment list to have only their known callers (`RequireFieldWriters` can't see writes through a getter). A wrong reuse found while building a snapshot rebuilds it with that part off (`SnapshotVerifier.Rebuilt`); found while computing its checksum, the checksum is computed again (`Reserialized`); before, the rest of that snapshot kept its shortcuts. A pile's header is the empty pile's bits with the count written in (it was serialized again, card by card, at each count change). Combat end releases every entry, not only those still in piles.
- **FastListeners/ListenerCache**: a MultiEnchantment verdict could be stale for one walk when a new inner type was noted during that walk, or (subscriber tracking off) never resynced: synced before and after building the snapshot, and on every path judging entries one by one. ListenerCache backs off on the walks' whole code (iterators' MoveNext, `Contains`, ModHelper's subscriber walks), not just `IterateHookListeners`; pile caching backs off while another mod patches `CardPile.Cards`, `CardModel.Enchantment` or `Affliction`; ModHelper's subscriber list pinned (fingerprint `4F5EA1DCEC53C6F9`, field writers). Kinds rescanned only for assemblies that can define models.
- **PileIndex**: Harmony never calls a prefix taking the card once an earlier one returned false, so "an add or remove another mod's prefix skipped, judged by the pile's count" only held for a prefix registered after PileIndex's. A first prefix that always runs now takes the count; the finalizer judges a skipped or thrown add/remove by the card's actual membership; cards added to a pile during its own `Clear` handled; checks run while switched off (a stale index faults for the session). **CardDbScan**: walks are the game's while another mod patches `AddInternal` (until the next combat start), an unseen add walks its pile in full; field writers of `NetCombatCardDb._cardToId`/`_nextId` pinned. **DeckCountMemo**: a card type whose `Tags`, `CanonicalStarCost` or `HasStarCostX` isn't a constant (IL check, per type, mods loaded later included; BaseLib's `ConstructedCardModel` counts as changing) or a patched `CardPile.Cards` gives the game's count.
- **Limitless patches**: only Limitless 1.2.1's known code (15 fingerprinted methods, load scan of Limitless and mods referencing it) may change the inner list `EnchantmentsOnCards` hands out; back-offs on the getter, `set_SerializableEnchantmentsOnCards` and (HookSkip drops) the clone-build chain. LimitlessCloneMemo's chain covers the enchantment side, `OnUpgrade`/`AfterCloned`, the dynamic-variable and energy-cost copies; its overrides list named the non-virtual `ModifyCard` (matched nothing): overrides on the way (`AfterDeserialized`, `DeepCloneFields`, `AfterCloned`, `OnUpgrade`, `OnEnchant`, `RecalculateValues`) are judged per saved type, only the game's or known code, unpatched. LimitlessRecalcSkip lets the game's pass run and watches it (an exception there passes on unchanged, no rerun); bare enchantments with their own `RecalculateValues` move the quiet state; `CardEntered` is a finalizer; memos see later SavedSpireFields. LimitlessHookSkip arms only with no combat set up, starting or running; one forwarded hook call in 16 checks a dropped stacked enchantment (`Samples`, `SampleMismatches`).
- **Hooks**: HookSkip's transpiler at `Priority.Last`; HookSkip no longer requires `IterateCombatHookListeners`' fingerprint (only its redirects depend on it, checked per walk); it runs `HarmonyRebuild.CheckAll` itself once its hooks are patched. A listener's own exception in a HookFilter mirror is rethrown as vanilla would (once, from the hook) without faulting HookFilter or calling earlier listeners again; logged with its trace first. ExtraScopes' bookkeeping can't break the async first-turn setup (fault, query without a scope); the BetterSpire2 scope pinned (5 methods) with a back-off; both scopes read their faults and a switch in `combat_refresh`. `classify.py`: "changed value" comparisons must be the listener's own before/after, `out`/`ref` arguments only the try identity or new declarations, PushModel/PopModel paired, listener-call arguments side-effect free, `TaskHelper.WhenAny` pinned (table unchanged on all three versions).
- **Infrastructure**: TextPoolReuse a part like the others (a fault was switched back on by `ApplyAll`); a faulted class whose patches can't be removed keeps its flags off and is retried after 2, 10 and 60 s; `[PatchPart]` covers the part method's lambdas, local functions and state machines; `IsPatchedByOthers` counts inner prefixes/postfixes. Scope and shuffle finalizers restore depth from `__state` (Harmony runs every finalizer again when one throws).
- **Smaller**: RoomCollect's fault restores the GC latency mode; CardFlightLimit's flush keeps the other piles' counts when one throws, and after a fault adds the full count; PoolSignalScan watches the explicit `INodePool.Free`; RelicStateReuse lets the game's `JsonException` through without faulting; `RunVanilla` (the snapshot verifier's and the benches' reference) is fully vanilla for FastListeners' mirror, the BaseLib card-modifier skip, PileIndex, DeckCountMemo and SavedPropertyLookup.

Test tooling (could pass while wrong):

- A failed `cmd` line only logged `FAILED:` and no judge read it (a failed `bigdeck import` ran both sides on the starter deck: IDENTICAL); self-detected mismatches and disables, TIMEOUT, SWEEP FAILED and 0-vs-0 comparisons passed; test slots inherited the user's `BigDeckOptimization.cfg` (an "on" session could run vanilla); no script compared a co-op session mod on with mod off (see "Game beta v0.113.1": the pairs even played different runs); `bdperf` vanilla sides kept optimizations on, `bdperf refresh` compared after the vanilla side had already refreshed; baselines ignored extra mods, run files and profile progress; `coop.ps1 -WithMods` could write into the Steam install. Now: one judge (`tools/autotest/judge.py`) for every runner, failed commands fail the session, positive controls declare `expect <regex>`, slots run with default settings, `parcoop.ps1 -OnOff` compares on with off per peer, `bdperf` vanilla = the whole mod off, refresh from a dirtied state, baselines keyed by loaded mods, run files and profile, effect and leak checks that can't read clean when inconclusive. Applied to old logs, the judge found a co-op ladder snapshot difference of 2026-11-02 nobody had seen, and scenarios whose `cmd` failed unseen (fixed: `audit-deckcount-fault` never added its card, `snapcheck-freeturn` played into a full hand...).

Found while proving it (test tooling):

- **Every effect proved**: `effects-check` never ran three of them. `SnapshotCardReuse.Hooks` (piles kept with pure cost/keyword listeners): Curious applied, three checksums. `ControllerActionSkip`: `controllermap stale` binds game actions to controller actions the InputMap lacks (what a v0.107 `settings.save` keeps; v0.111+ drop such bindings themselves, `MergeSavedControllerBindings`), then a mouse sweep. `SyncedCardRelease` is co-op only: `effects-coop-host/-client` (parcoop). Solo: 55/56 on v0.111/v0.113, 54/56 on v0.107.1, where `ReplayStateSkip` can't run solo (v0.107 enables its `ChecksumTracker` in co-op only; v0.111+ checksum a solo combat too); co-op: both run (v0.113.1 1,903 releases and 78 skipped states per peer). HookFilter's effect counts the listeners its lists leave out (`ListenersDropped`), not lists built; HookSkip's filtered walks are counted as before, what they leave out is `FastListeners.Kinds/.Others`.
- **`play` in co-op**: the card goes through the networked action queue; the player looked idle before it ran and the next pick took the same card (the queue then dropped it). How fast the queue runs depends on the session: a mod-off client played 1 card of 3. `play` now waits until the combat history saw that card's play start (a set: the other player's plays start in between).
- **Co-op on/off needs turns**: both players playing at once interleave their actions in the shared queue at each peer's pace; random targets then land differently (same total damage) between a mod-on and a mod-off session (v0.107.1). `effects-coop` and `coopfast` take turns within a round (barriers) and wait with `waitfor round n` (both peers ended the turn), not `waitfor turn` (a client's end of turn reaches its queue only with the host's echo, so its next `waitfor turn` could still see the turn it had just ended, and it played a fourth card in round 1 when the host was slower; since the fourth pass the `endturn` line itself waits until the game took the end of turn). Concurrent play stays covered by the peers' checksums after every action; `audit-coopdeath` (a player dies, both play at once) is peers-only.
- **`autoroom`**: the game's event handler can call an event done before its rewards open (THE_LEGENDS_WERE_TRUE's potion, host, v0.113.1): a screen open with no option is now answered. `outside-coop`/`-off` run alone (`-Jobs 2`): mod off, the 1,900-card combat start took 237 s with other sessions running and the client got disconnected (the vanilla freeze).
- **Positive controls by chance**: `relics-scalarblind` relied on the part's 1-in-16 sample falling on a relic that really changed (none did on v0.107.1), and whichever of its two detectors saw the error first hid it from the other (the verifier saw nothing on v0.111.0). Split: `relics-scalarblind` (every answer checked, the part turns off) and `relics-scalarblind-verifier` (the part's sample out of reach, the verifier sees a snapshot differ). `snapcheck-apotheosis-wrong` now also expects the checksum computed again.
- **Benches**: a part not applied (the game's code changed: a mutation, another version) says "not applied: nothing to compare", without "OK" and without failing; applied but off still fails. `baselib-optional.ps1` accepts the two hooks HookSkip leaves vanilla on v0.113 by design; `started-off-nomenu.ps1`: a saved "off" with the menu DLL set aside starts on, patches applied.
- **mutations.ps1**: `SnapshotCardReuse.Hooks` goes with Trust/Moved/Dirty; a changed Limitless forwarder or `RecalculateValues` is a known inner-list writer, so LimitlessHookSkip and the pass skipping go vanilla whole; bdperf comparisons only (not ExtraScopes' first-turn line), parts left vanilla excused.
- A mod-off co-op client crashed once in the game's Spine animation call (native access violation in `MegaSpineBinding.Call`, under a Thorns damage chain, BaseLib's `SetAnimationTrigger` postfix on the way; no frame of this mod there): not reproduced on a rerun.

Proof (Debug, BaseLib 3.4.8, Limitless 1.2.1, the same DLL on v0.107.1, v0.111.0 and v0.113.1): `tools/compat` 0 problem on the four refs; `release-checks` 0 part left vanilla on v0.107.1/v0.111.0, the two known hooks on v0.113.1. On each version: `ab-2k` 54, `pile-cards-2k` 131, `combats3-2k` 63, `shuffle` 55, `clone` 4, `deckcount` 131 lines identical (`fool-2k` 153 on v0.113.1), `autoslay-2k` 613 / 387 / 747, `memory-runs` 32, `outside-2k` 48 (v0.107.1, v0.111.0) identical; every positive control saw its error, judge 0 problem; with every patched method rebuilt (`BDTEST_REBUILD_ALL=1`) `ab-2k` identical and `rebuild-check` ok; co-op `effects-coop` and `coopfast` on/off identical per peer (56 / 55 lines), `outside-coop` on/off 30 lines identical per peer, `coopclone` and `audit-coopdeath` peers agree; `settings`, `baselib-optional`, `limitless-switches`, `started-off`, `started-off-nomenu` ok. Mutations 38/38 (public). `turns-32k-on` 31,800 cards: 681 passes skipped, verify mismatches 0. `hover-tips-8k` rendered: no problem. Sweeps (v0.113.1 / v0.107.1): cards 4,269 / 3,452, relics 4,740 / 4,455, potions 348 / 328, encounters 3,321 / 3,146, powers 6,139 / 5,240 lines identical (powers stopping a session do so mod off too).

### Audit of 2026-11-15, second and third passes

Two more full passes, nine read-only auditors each (the test tooling split in C# and scripts), each reading its part, the game and mod code it touches on the three versions, and the uncommitted diff of the passes before; the third pass started without the earlier findings. No crash, desync or state difference with today's game and mods in either; what they found were holes a game update or another mod could open, and test tooling that could pass while wrong. `decompiled/mods/BaseLib` was BaseLib 3.4.7 and `BetterSpire2` a 1.0.0 build: now the installed 3.4.8 and Workshop 1.83.14 (old copies kept as `BaseLib-3.4.7`, `BetterSpire2-1.0.0`); `LobbyBanlist` (Workshop 3769761568, in the account's mod list since 2026-11-08: every test session loads it; its initializer throws on v0.113, it loads on v0.107.1) decompiled there too: its `NCardGrid.SortingAlgorithms` postfix and GridSortCache's give the game's order in either order, nothing else it patches is something this mod mirrors or relies on.

Real today (small): **IdleButtonInput** tracked only the mutes `NClickableControl.Enable` asked for, not those it made itself (at ready, after a release or a drag-cancel): turned off, most idle buttons never got their input back (nothing visible in the game; another mod's `_Input` patch would not have run). Every mute is tracked now.

Holes closed (a game update or another mod's patch):

- **Harmony skips a prefix taking a reference-type argument once an earlier prefix returned false** (checked in the game's 0Harmony 2.4.2): FastListeners' add/remove/reorder/affliction prefixes now leave a "never ran" state their finalizers turn into a full invalidation, and the caches back off while another mod patches `AddInternal`, `RemoveInternal`, `MoveToBottom/TopInternal` or `set_Affliction`; HookSkip's pending run filter is taken by an always-run prefix (`Priority.First`, no argument). PileIndex: a card mid-add or mid-remove gets the game's scan while another mod patches those; `Judge` faults instead of restoring over a nested change (an `Entry.Changes` counter), decides from membership, never counts; a `Clear` counts its own removes.
- **SnapshotCardReuse**: a replaced snapshot object invalidates the card's memo; a relic detection rebuilds the snapshot like a card one (`RelicStateReuse.Detections`); no kept bits for saved cards, for card arrays of SavedSpireField targets, or for an object whose BaseLib saves hand out a collection or an object; the getters a trusted pile skips (`Id`, `Affliction`, `Enchantment`, `CurrentUpgradeLevel`, `FloorAddedToDeck`, `EnergyCost`, the amounts, `Canonical`, `CostsX`, `Type`, `Owner`, `Pile`) back off on another mod's patch; `CardModel.Type` pinned (fingerprint, field writers) and card types overriding it read at every snapshot; `_localModifiers` and `LocalCostModifier.Amount` users pinned; the pile-bits check no longer delayed, its comments say what it covers (the per-card bits are the 1-in-2048 check's).
- **DeckCountMemo**: moves applied only from a count taken with no changing card in the piles; per-type patched-getter flags rechecked every 2 s; `AllCards` fingerprinted.
- **Limitless**: the `EnchantmentsOnCards` getter's fingerprint required before any drop; the game's clone and build chain pinned (25 methods, `GameChain`; v0.113's changed `CardModel.DeepCloneFields`, `AfterCloned`, `UpgradeInternal` reviewed as equivalent) and the six game overrides on it (`Adroit.RecalculateValues`, five `OnEnchant`; game card types with any override on the way are no longer trusted); assemblies loaded later that reference Limitless are scanned for inner-list writers; the pass skip backs off on `AllCards` patches; `CardEntered` checks `Holds`.
- **Infrastructure**: InlineGuard judges a patched method by what Harmony compiles (transpilers rerun, every mod's patch methods scanned, the JIT's inlining rules); `ApplyAll` sets each flag in its own try and schedules a faulted removal first; a removal failing at apply faults and retries; `tools/compat` shares the load check's accessibility and part rules; back-offs are recorded for every caller (the menu names them all); `check_versions.py` watches BetterSpire2, SpeedX and Unlimited.
- **Smaller**: ListenerCache invalidates through finalizers (a watched method that throws after changing its list), skips the BaseLib card-modifier subscriber only when nobody patches it, and a move-check mismatch reads the piles again; CardDbScan rescans a pile it starts tracking from a walk; CardFlightLimit leaves a null target pile to the game; ThemeColorSkip leaves a freed label to the game's call; ExtraScopes' first-turn part left vanilla now shows as not applied; `HookSkip.Patched` (awaited by nothing) removed.

**HookSkip now filters every v0.113 hook**: `classify.py` proves the four shapes it left to the game (AfterTakingExtraTurn's `if (model == source)`, ModifyUnblockedDamageTarget's and ModifyPreBlockDamageTarget's `combatState?.IterateHookListeners() ?? Array.Empty<AbstractModel>()` walk, TryReplaceDefaultRoomRewards' per-listener list), and requires what it had accepted unchecked (the `netId` guard, task lists only awaited by `WhenAll`, property chains reviewed one by one). v0.113.1: 154 hooks filtered, 0 left vanilla; the v0.107.1 and v0.111.0 rows unchanged.

Test tooling (could pass while wrong): a networked `cmd` in co-op couldn't fail (its result went to the console only; its task's fault too); `restsite`, `eventoption`, `playcard`, `autoroom` skipped a missing option or card silently (`optional` opts in); sweep refusals were unjudged; the judge ignored the game's `State divergence`, mod frames in game and Godot errors, a failed AutoSlay run, its own crash; `run.ps1` could judge a stale log and killed any SlayTheSpire2 process (yours included) on a stall; `try.ps1` deployed to the Steam install unasked; baselines cached partly-off scenarios; co-op on/off compared no checksum; benches said OK without their optimized path having run (refresh, grid sort/pool, tabs, listeners, pile, plaque, text, checksum rows: now `nothing to compare` / `not compared`, never OK); several effect counters counted when nothing was done; `checktips` compared text only (now the panel's input and placement against the game's own rebuild for the same hitbox); off sides of A/Bs now start vanilla (`bdopt off` before the run is created); `mods_STEAMTEST` shared per copy is reference-counted and each runner checks the mods loaded; restarted sessions, `-Chunks` reruns and sweep coverage are judged; `coop.ps1` runs in a test slot.

Found while proving it: the game's **"Common" preload** at the main menu is not awaited; a run started meanwhile (a script 0.4 s after the menu) unloads what it is still loading and the run's preload never completes, mod on or off (two sessions stopped there): sessions now wait for it (0.7 s). A co-op host's `bdopt off` at the menu is saved in the slot both peers share, so the off client may start with the mod off (judge `--mod-off` on off sides). On v0.111.0 `outside-coop`'s path meets Abyssal Baths: the game applies a remote player's event choice to that player's options at arrival without waiting for the previous choice's async work (`EventSynchronizer.ChooseOptionForEvent`); Immerse awaits max HP and damage before changing page, the auto player clicks Exit at once, and the client sometimes applied it as Abstain (a heal) to the host's player: 3 sessions of 3 mod on, 0 of 3 mod off, the client's view right again at the next state line (remote players are synced from the host). Measured frame by frame (`eventtrace on`, both peers, on and off): Immerse's damage awaits the game's own hit `CustomScaledWait` (88-106 ms, on both peers, mod on and off alike: not skipped outside combat); the host sends Exit right after its own wait, the client finishes the host's Immerse after its copy of that wait, and the second choice arrived only 21 ms (on) and 14 ms (off) after the page change: a game race won or lost by a frame, whatever the mod. With the trace's own logging both sides won. Nothing the mod does there differs; the auto player's instant clicks make it possible, a player's can't. On v0.113.1 `outside-2k`'s rest site clones the deck to 6,655 cards and mod off the next combat start takes over 7 minutes (`automap` gives up): compared up to there.

Proof (Debug, the same DLL on v0.107.1, v0.111.0 and v0.113.1): `tools/compat` 0 problem on the four refs; `release-checks` 0 part left vanilla on all three (HookSkip 147 / 148 / 154 hooks). On each version: `ab-2k` 54, `pile-cards-2k` 131, `combats3-2k` 63, `shuffle` 55, `clone` 4, `deckcount` 131 lines identical (`fool-2k` 153 on v0.113.1), `memory-runs` 32, `autoslay-2k` 613 / 387 / 747, `outside-2k` 48 / 48 / 42 (to the freeze above) identical; every positive control and audit scenario passed (new: `audit-idlebuttons`, `audit-deckcount-conflict`, `hover-tips-move`), judge 0 problem; with every patched method rebuilt `ab-2k` identical and `rebuild-check` ok; `effects-check` 55/56 (54 on v0.107.1), `effects-coop` gives the co-op ones (SyncedCardRelease 1,903 per peer, ReplayStateSkip 103 / 105); co-op `effects-coop` and `coopfast` on/off identical per peer (58 / 58 / 57 lines with the settled checksums), `outside-coop` on/off 30 lines (the v0.111.0 race above aside), `coopclone`, `audit-coopdeath`, `audit-cooptoggle` peers agree, `audit-waitspin` (plain) 2,001 waits then the frame given back; `settings`, `baselib-optional` (no excused hook left), `limitless-switches`, `started-off`, `started-off-nomenu` ok; `hover-tips-move` and `hover-tips-8k` rendered on all three: input and placement OK. Mutations 40/40 (public, new: `clone-from`, `limitless-getter`). `turns-32k-on` (public): 681 passes skipped, verify mismatches 0. Sweeps without baselines (v0.113.1 / v0.107.1): cards 4,269 / 3,452, relics 4,740 / 4,455, potions 348 / 328, encounters 3,321 / 3,146, powers 6,139 / 5,240 lines identical.

### Audit of 2026-11-15, fourth pass

Sixteen read-only auditors (listener walks; listener cache and hook filter; hook skipping and `classify.py`; snapshots; relics and net saves; the Limitless patches in two groups; piles, card ids and sorting; combat flow and GC; UI; infrastructure and settings; the test tooling in three groups: AutoTest, benches, scripts; tools and release; the docs), each reading every line of its part and the game and mod code it touches on v0.107.1, v0.111.0, v0.113.0 and v0.113.1, then an adversarial verifier per finding (confirmed, narrowed or rejected, often with a better fix) and a critic over the whole. The same groups fixed their own files, then a review of each group's diff. No crash, desync or state difference with today's game and mods; what was found: a few real bugs, most of them small or in the test tooling, holes a game update or another mod could open, and many stale docs. During the audit the Steam install switched to the `public-beta` branch (v0.113.1), Unlimited updated to 2.0.0, and every Workshop mod was disabled in the account's `settings.save` (the user's own doing; see the test mods below).

Real today:

- **Turning the mod off in a co-op run** (the user's request): `bdopt off` also turns off the fast combat start the other players keep, so this player starts each combat far later and the game drops the actions reaching a player still starting a combat. During a co-op run `bdopt off` now prints a warning and needs `bdopt off yes`; the menu's master switch can't be unticked there (greyed, its hover text says why and names `bdopt off yes`, 14 languages); `bdopt` warns while the mod is off in a co-op run; the menu's co-op note says so; a FastCombatStart fault in co-op is logged as such. Not detected: a co-op lobby before the run starts (no static access to it). Co-op players must now all have the mod on, or all off (`CoopModeTag`): with the whole mod off, this mod's entry in the game's gameplay mod list reads `BigDeckOptimization-<version>-off`, so the game's own join check refuses mixing on and off players ("mod mismatch", naming both); two off players can play, anyone can host. `bdperf opt off` asks the same; `bdperf costwrites` (a cost change on one peer) is refused in co-op.
- **Stack traces**: 25 pass-through finalizers returned the exception. Harmony 2.4 rethrows unchanged only when every finalizer on the method is void, so every exception through those methods, the game's and other mods' included, lost its trace, even with `bdopt off` (the patches stay installed). Now void; only `HookFilter.RethrowFinalizer` returns one (it substitutes an exception, and logs the full trace first).
- **`bdopt` was a debug command**: on v0.113 using it (even to read the state) marked the run as having used a debug command in its save (`ExtraFields.MarkDebugToolUsed`). Now `DebugOnly => false`.
- **ListenerCache**: a hand selection ended by a state change resumes the card's play inline, inside the `CombatStateChanged` dispatch scope, with that scope's cached lists: `NPlayerHand.RevalidateSelectionAfterStateChange` now suspends the caches. Kept display lists held a finished combat until the next reuse: dropped at every invalidation.
- **HoverTipReuse**: with the mouse resting on a creature while a keyboard or controller opened a potion popup (or targeting), a co-op state change kept the creature's panel on screen where the game removes it (`NHoverTipSet.shouldBlockHoverTips`, `NGame.IsDebugHidingHoverTips`: the game's rebuild runs then).
- **ModImage** ignored the master switch: `bdopt off` kept the mod's image on the mods screen. An image Godot can't decode was read again and logged at every fill.
- **Menu**: the fast-start relic slider stayed active when `fast_combat_start` couldn't run (now greyed, without input, with that option's reason); the co-op grey-out compared a `StringName` with a string. `coop_checksum`'s description now says it also runs in solo on v0.111+.
- **Costs at run time**: `PatchSafety`'s 2-s recheck read each foreign patch's method through `AccessTools.GetMethodByModuleAndToken` (a `Parallel.ForEach` over every loaded assembly), on the main thread, mid-combat: now resolved once per (module, token). LimitlessCloneMemo's stamp read Limitless's two option getters (each a reflective `RunManager` call) for every inner card, ~60,000 at a 2 x 10,000-card combat start: now once per outermost `DeepCloneFields`. RelicStateReuse compiled `IsWax`/`IsMelted` getters again for every relic type (hundreds of compiles in one co-op checksum, estimated): now once per getter. GcPacing recounted a combat under 1,000 cards every frame, boxing enumerators. GridSortCache read a `ConditionalWeakTable` at every comparison (~260,000 per 10,000-card two-key sort).
- **Effect counters that counted nothing**: GcPacing's `Regions` counted refused regions; FastCombatStart's skipped waits counted waits at Instant speed or in the game's non-interactive mode; HookSkip's `FilteredWalks` counted NullRunState walks; ReplayStateSkip's `Skipped` counted calls where nothing was recording (it also skipped there, hiding the game's own error: now the game's method runs).

Holes closed (a game update or another mod):

- **Listener walks** (FastListeners): appends have their own switch, off with a changed `AddInternal` or a move-check mismatch (before, only shifted moves were); an exception in this mod's verdicts inside a filtered walk (kinds, relics, per-entry judgments, the version tails) turns FastListeners off and refills the walk unfiltered, as the pile entry cache did, and the kinds scan fails safe (keeps every kind); `set_Enchantment`'s tracking uses a byte state (a prefix that never ran bumps in the finalizer); shuffle depth stored + 1 (a skipped prefix can't reset an outer shuffle); the mirrored subscriber walk throws the game's "Collection was modified" when a subscription is added mid-walk.
- **ListenerCache / HookFilter / ExtraScopes**: creature lists and powers watched directly (six optional finalizers) instead of relying on a watched change running just before; cross-scope reuse refused while a concrete BaseLib `CardModifier` exists; scopes inert unless `Apply` finished; scope depth + 1 in `__state` (0 closes nothing: before, a skipped prefix could end the enclosing scopes). HookFilter's energy cost, ShouldPlay and hitting mirrors take the game's early exits before building a list; only the hooks walking `Hook.IterateCombatHookListeners` back off on patches to it and its getters (the energy cost color helper still does: shared lists, deferred).
- **HookSkip**: a hook's fingerprint compared exactly with its table row (an equivalence added for another patch could have let an unclassified body through); the async helpers' state machines checked for other mods' patches; a pending BaseLib run filter that NullRunState never took is dropped by a first-priority prefix; a game `Hook` method with no row that walks the listeners is counted and logged as left vanilla (v0.113.0's release checks said "two left vanilla" while four were unsafe: the two without a row went uncounted; 0 today on all four versions); BaseLib's allowed patches fingerprinted at the menu, not in a run's first hook. Accepted and documented: subscribers judged when the walk's list is built (UI only), and the transpiler's place among equal priorities registered later.
- **Snapshots** (SnapshotCardReuse, SnapshotVerifier): the reuse check compares bit lengths too (1 to 7 extra trailing zero bits shift the checksum stream); the verifier's vanilla side is serialized inside `RunVanilla`, through the game's own `WriteList`, so the mirrored list header is really checked; an overloaded hook name in a content mod no longer turns the part off (`AmbiguousMatchException`); a listener's own exception in the calls the game's `From` also makes lets the game's `From` run and throw it, without faulting the part (`GameExceptions`); `WriteModelEntry` and `WriteInt` in the card bits' back-off (the upgrade bits write into them); a card with a BaseLib custom keyword keeps its kept keyword list; pile bits require exactly one replaced `WriteList<CombatPileState>`; the verifier's size check inside its try.
- **Relics** (RelicStateReuse): Unlimited's transpiler resolved on its own, so a change elsewhere in RelicStateReuse no longer removes SnapshotCardReuse's allowance for Unlimited; patches on `SavedProperties.Serialize` / `RelicState.Serialize` back off; a type whose scalar plan can't be built is left to the game instead of turning the scalar part off and counting a detection; skipped parts logged with the shared "left vanilla" wording (`check_versions --log` sees them).
- **Limitless**: the pass skip checks the saved list's writers and getter callers; the list shortcut only for `AlwaysSave` saved properties; a late Limitless load applies only what isn't applied; off-main-thread bumps counted apart in the quiet word (a real race in the first fix: one atomic word now); duplicate BaseLib save ids counted once; **the kept-list self-check runs in every build** (it was Debug only and only logged): a difference turns LimitlessReserialize off and Limitless rebuilds the list. LimitlessCloneMemo: a mod type declaring an instance `[SavedProperty]` is never kept; the back-off chain goes one level down (keywords, `NCard.FindOnTable`, energy cost and dynamic variable upgrades, `MaxUpgradeLevel`; the known BaseLib and Limitless patches there allowed); lock-free per-type answers. LimitlessHookSkip: any use of the inner or saved list's members (matched by handle) outside the known code counts as a writer. EnchantmentTabReuse: three more methods its mirror relies on fingerprinted; an orphan duplicate freed on an error.
- **Piles and sorting**: PileCounterBatch's and PileIndex's `Clear` finalizers survive Harmony rerunning every finalizer when one throws (a depth at -1 froze the counters; a rerun faulted the index); `InNoPile` unsure for a card in flight; `ContainsCard` gets its own flag (`PileIndex.ContainsCardEnabled`, set by `pile_lookup` with the master switch, not by PileIndex's faults: the shortcut reads no index). CardSortKeys: where a cost listener reads `card.Keywords` (Veilpiercer), every keyword override in the combat must be a fingerprinted adder (Hex, Phantom Blades) no other mod patches, the keyword code pinned (the first version of this fix refused every sort in a Veilpiercer combat, found by its review); off-main-thread comparisons are the game's; a type re-implementing the comparison interface is refused.
- **Combat flow and GC**: FastCombatStart leaves a wait that is no longer async to the game alone (it lost the whole patch); RoomCollect counts its replaced `GC.Collect()` calls and skips itself with none, before setting the latency mode; CardFlightLimit re-queues a lost deferred flush; RichTextEnvRelease covers v0.111's `[scramble]` effect, and a failing tracker part comes off alone; TextPoolReuse pins the method that makes the shared pool's dirty buffers safe.
- **UI**: PoolSignalScan mirrors only the game's `NodePool<>` (BaseLib's `GeneratedNodePool<T>` or a mod's own pool could fault it) and backs off on a patch on any pool; IdleButtonInput pins the writers of `_isPressed`; GridSortCache pins `SetCards` for its index map.
- **Infrastructure**: `HarmonyRebuild` refuses methods carrying a transpiler known to keep state between runs (BaseLib's `ModInterop`); InlineGuard records protected methods even when its flag probe fails (MonoMod sets the flag anyway: the recompile is what matters), follows callees 4 levels deep; patches applied at once still get the recompile and `CheckAll` when frames can't be awaited.

Test tooling (could pass while wrong):

- **The test mods**: with every Workshop mod disabled in the account's settings, every copy session would have run with this mod alone and every A/B agreed. The supported mods (BaseLib, Limitless, BetterSpire2) now always load, plus the account's enabled mods and `BDTEST_EXTRA_MODS` (`BDTEST_MODS` replaces the list); a wanted mod missing stops the series; each test mod must log its initialization (an exception in it, or Unlimited's `loaded DEGRADED`, is a problem; LobbyBanlist's v0.113 failure a known note); slot profiles enable every mod of the test mods' folders.
- **Judges**: every `[WARN]` of this mod is a problem unless allow-listed; compare.py and sweepreport.py count game errors logged only, or far more often, with the mod on; frozen sessions keep their log (`.frozenN`) and a freeze in this mod's load is a problem; a cut-short session isn't held to `expect` lines it never reached; Test-SessionLog fails a negative check on a missing or cut-short log; exit codes for every runner; back-off scenarios assert each back-off (`expect`, `effectcheck ... zero`); the effects check lists CardSortKeys wherever needed (`EFFECT CardSortKeys 0` when it should run and didn't) and leaves the tooling's own `InlineGuard` protections out; benches restore the effect counters and refuse a part faulted earlier; `bdperf refresh`/`scan`/grid say `not compared` when nothing of this mod ran.
- **Script lines**: AutoSlay's 10-minute wait stretch no longer applies to the tooling's own waits (60/120 s); `endturn` waits until the game took it (a co-op client's next lines landed in the turn just ended); in co-op `enemyhp`/`immortal` fail after this peer's combat was set up (barrier missing: before, a divergence that looked like the mod's); only the game's Error lines set the turn-loop and divergence flags; the v0.113 fight seed logged; `selector local` on v0.113; long waits log their state at 180 s; a sweep abort stops the script; frame times kept only while measured; `leakcheck` weak tables on request (not judged: a value pointing back to its own key keeps nothing alive) and `leakcheck game` paths through this mod's objects marked `[through this mod: Type]`, a problem for judge.py; a `state` line outside a combat fails unless `optional` (`StateOutsideCombatFails`; clone-on/off, cloneopt-on/off and audit-coopdeath-client say it where they log outside a combat on purpose).
- **Runners**: run.ps1 refuses the Steam install without a Debug build there and closes a game whose runner logs nothing in 90 s; co-op ports per test slot; bounded robocopy retries; baselines hash every file of the test mods' folders and `BigDeckConsoleCmd.cs`; mutations.ps1 stops on a build failure, counts an undefined mutation, never excuses a runtime fault, runs one at a time; python reports never crash on a character the code page lacks.
- **Versions and release**: `check_versions.py` reads the installed branch (with Steam on `public-beta` every daily check said "game CHANGED", and `--accept` would have recorded the beta as the public game; verified betas now in `tools/versions.json` `betas`); `--accept` records only what is installed and refuses while a published update isn't downloaded (the v1.0.3 commit had recorded Unlimited v2.0.0's publish stamp against v1.0.3: back to 1.0.3's); `--log` knows every fallback phrase and lists every error and warning line of the mod; a crash exits 2 and `version_task.ps1` notifies it. `fpdiff` loads every pinned mod and says which it didn't find (its one-off `migrate` removed). `package.py` builds from committed sources (Source Link names HEAD; `--draft` otherwise), not incremental, against `refs/v<min_game_version>`, with the csproj version checked against the manifest and every menu key required in every translation. The no-Steam-deploy hook judges each command of a compound line and builds inside other commands. `dwarfsyms` warns on DWARF 5 units.

Docs: DESIGN's intro, implemented-patches rows (ListenerCache's invalidators, HookSkip's counts and scope, LimitlessRecalcSkip's pass skip and its setter dependency, PileIndex's judging, CardDbScan's fingerprints, SnapshotCardReuse's patches...), console commands (all 20 option keys), test tooling and roadmap brought in line with the code; `docs/RELEASE.md` gets the version check protocol (it pointed to CLAUDE.md, which isn't in the repository).

Deferred, with the reason:

- FastListeners' warm-up of the kinds scan at load: unmeasured, the first run-start hook to be measured first. LimitlessReserialize's memo shortcut for forwarded hooks: unmeasured. Clearing LimitlessCloneMemo at run end: bounded and reused by the next run.
- SnapshotCardReuse: an error in `card.ToSerializable()` still faults the part (avoiding it needs a transpiler on `CardState.From`'s hot path, for an error that already breaks vanilla's checksum); `Reads` cached per pile, not per combat (cheap lookups).
- HookFilter: the energy cost color helper still backs off on a patched combat walk (lists shared with SnapshotCardReuse). InlineGuard: no recompile after a late apply (~300 ms of rebuilds mid-run). LimitlessHookSkip's known limit stays (only another mod can reach it). BaseLib's `RandomCardUpgradeReward` sorts with the game's comparison (no listed mod grants it). CardFlightLimit's null soul relies on the game's test-mode contract, not a fingerprint (the version review greps the callers).
- Wiring across files, done after the fixers: `ModEntry.NotApplied` reads `RelicStateReuse.ScalarNotApplied`; `ModEntry.TryApply` applies a name once (a second call, e.g. a late Limitless load, logs "already applied" and touches nothing: a second Apply under the same Harmony id would patch twice, and a failing one would remove the first one's patches); Settings sets `PileIndex.ContainsCardEnabled` from `pile_lookup`; `gc_pacing`'s text says up to about 1.4 GB (14 languages); `bdperf opt` is never saved (it changes only the session's `Settings.Master`; BaseLib saves `Settings.MasterChoice`, the player's choice: BaseLib's save of every config on `NGame.Quit` recorded a `bdperf opt off` and the next launch started off); `MethodFingerprint.Mutators` counts the `HashSet` in-place set operations (`UnionWith`, `ExceptWith`, `IntersectWith`, `SymmetricExceptWith`, `RemoveWhere`) as writes (the only `HashSet` fields pinned, `CardModel._keywords` and `_tags`, have no other user on the four versions: nothing goes vanilla); the csproj refuses a deploy into the Steam install unless `-p:AllowSteamDeploy=true` (the hands-on demo script passes it). Still open: co-op barriers before `enemyhp`; `play n` failing on 0 plays, kept passing ("played 0" after an empty hand or nothing playable is a normal result in about 40 current logs: it needs an `optional` audit of the 118 `play` scenarios first).
- Unlimited 2.0.0: reviewed, not accepted. The pinned fingerprints (`908CDF014E4F04CF`, `CB9DCCF74112728F`) are unchanged; its new `ListHeaderWidthPatch` prefixes `WriteList`/`ReadList` only for the lobby's struct lists (closed value-type code, not shared with `WriteList<CardState>`/`<CombatPileState>`, and it only widens `lengthBits == 3`); its difficulty patches touch nothing this mod mirrors. Its `ApplyHarmonyPatches` references v0.113-only screens, so on v0.107.1/v0.111.0 it may fail to apply its own patches. Remaining: one `BDTEST_EXTRA_MODS` battery on v0.107.1 and v0.113.1 (v0.111.0 no longer tested, "Technical choices"), then `--accept`.

Proof: pending (no game session yet). To run (sessions on v0.107.1 and v0.113.1: v0.111.0 is no longer tested since 2026-11-16, "Technical choices"): `tools/compat` on the four refs (static, v0.111.0's too); `release-checks` (0 left vanilla, HookSkip 147 / 154, `LeftVanilla` 0); `effects-check` and `effects-coop` (the new counters: `ListenerCache.CreatureInvalidations` > 0, `HookSkip.PendingFiltersDropped` 0, `LimitlessReserialize.Verified` > 0, `LimitlessCloneMemo.OptionsReused` > 0, `RelicStateReuse.GettersCompiled` a few dozen, `PileIndex.FastContainsCards`); `ab-2k`, `pile-cards-2k`, `combats3-2k`, `shuffle`, `clone`, `deckcount`, `fool-2k`, `outside-2k`, `autoslay-2k` identical on every version; the audit scenarios and positive controls (`audit-conflict-ab`, `-saves`, `-walk-conflict`, `-parts-visible`, `conflict-check`, `rebuild-check` with its forced warning, `sortkeys-wrong`, `relics-scalarblind`); a Veilpiercer combat on v0.113 (CardSortKeys answers, no refusal; `bdperf sortkeys` now adds a Veilpiercer case on v0.113+); `bdperf fault walk`, `bdperf snapgameerror <n>`, `bdperf reserialize verify <n>`, `rebuildcheck stateful X.M`; a co-op join with one player's mod on and the other's off (refused, "mod mismatch") and both off (joins); co-op `coopfast` and `effects-coop` on/off, `audit-cooptoggle` with `bdopt off yes`, a by-hand check of `bdopt off` without `yes` in a co-op run (warning, mod stays on) and of the menu's greyed switch and slider; `memory-runs` and `coopleak`; `mutations.ps1`; `bdperf grid`, `bdperf segments verify all`; the release package in game on each copy.

### Game beta v0.113: sorting cards before a shuffle (2026-11-15, `CardSortKeys`)

v0.113 extended `CardModel.CompareTo`: after the id (`AbstractModel.CompareTo`) and the upgrade level, the enchantment and affliction ids (`ModelId.Compare`), `EnergyCost.GetResolved()` (in a combat pile: `Hook.ModifyEnergyCostInCombat`, two walks of every listener), `BaseReplayCount`, then each dynamic variable in `DynamicVars.Keys.Order(StringComparer.Ordinal)` order (allocated and sorted at each call). `ListExtensions.StableShuffle` sorts a copy of its list with it before shuffling: every draw pile reshuffle (`CardPileCmd.Shuffle`), Stone Cracker's `AfterRoomEntered` and 13 more card, relic and event methods. dotnet-trace of a combat start at ~1,900 cards on v0.113.1, mod on: `AfterRoomEntered` 959 ms, Stone Cracker's sort 856 ms of it; 6.5 s with 4 players (a co-op client then timed out). On v0.107.1 and v0.111.0 the comparison is the id and upgrade level only.

- **What**: inside a `StableShuffle` of a `List<CardModel>`, a prefix on `CardModel.CompareTo` answers with the same logic, each value read live but two computed once per card for that sort: the resolved cost and the ordered key list. The list's `Sort` is the game's: the same answers give the same order, bit for bit.
- **The scope**: a Harmony patch on `StableShuffle<CardModel>` itself is not possible: a generic method's code is shared by every reference type, Harmony compiles the replacement for the one instantiation it is given and detours the shared code, and the next `StableShuffle` of another type ran the `CardModel` replacement (checked outside the game with its 0Harmony 2.4.2 on .NET 9, the same method shape: `EntryPointNotFoundException` in `List<T>(IEnumerable<T>)` for the other type; in the game, the map's, events' and relics' shuffles of other types). Instead the 15 methods of v0.113.1 that call `StableShuffle<CardModel>` (found at load by scanning 15 listed types, state machines included; the whole assembly has no other caller) get a transpiler (`Priority.Last`) replacing that call with `CardSortKeys.StableShuffleInScope`, which opens the scope and calls the game's `StableShuffle`. PunchOfJoy and EcstasyPower shuffle a `List<Sadness>`: left alone. A caller a future version adds stays the game's.
- **Exact because nothing changes during the sort**: a scope opens only when every card's runtime type keeps `CardModel.CompareTo` (no override, no comparison interface of its own: the game would run other code) and, for each combat the cards are in (`CardModel.CombatState`, read once per card; a card in no combat pile runs no cost hook, `GetWithModifiers`' own test), every listener of that combat overriding `TryModifyEnergyCostInCombat(Late)` is a known pure one (`SnapshotCardReuse.PureHooks`, same fingerprints on v0.113, now internal) not patched by another mod, and the combat hook walk is the known one (`HookFilter.CombatHookWalkPatched`). Then a comparison only reads: the costs and keys can't change between two comparisons, and the cards' lazily created `EnergyCost` and `DynamicVars` are created at the same comparison as in the game (same reads, same order). Otherwise the whole sort is the game's (`Refused`, `LastRefusal`). Not kept: the ordered keys alone while costs stay live, which a side-effecting cost listener could make stale, and which saves little next to the cost walks.
- **What else could make two `GetResolved` of a card differ within a sort**: only a cost listener with side effects, or one reading something that changes during the sort; nothing else runs (the sort is synchronous; scopes open on the main thread only). The cost's local modifiers (`LocalCostModifier.Modify`) and `CostsX`/captured X value are fields. Excluded through the listener rule (Void Form, Brilliant Scarf, Ensemble, Free Card, and v0.113's Cho Limbs and Coda count the turn's plays: not in `PureHooks`, so their combats keep the game's sort; they only read, so a list of read-only listeners could extend this later).
- **Safety**: fingerprints required (v0.113.0 = v0.113.1): `CardModel.CompareTo` `F6388632B42CF646` (the v0.107.1/v0.111.0 one, `2CC3990F98A5EF89`, makes it "not needed": skipped, logged, not shown as missing: `OptionPart.NotNeeded`), `AbstractModel.CompareTo` `BB460DAC433857B6`, `ModelId.Compare` `9044DFF12964F53B` (called through a delegate: v0.113 only), `ListExtensions.StableShuffle` `68E6CDB3F3082D3C`, `CardEnergyCost.GetResolved` `5FAA75114FDAFC70` and `GetWithModifiers` `60FE75B459B24CFC`, `LocalCostModifier.Modify` `E11E84E9412802B6`, `Hook.ModifyEnergyCostInCombat` `081395225799F6BD`, the two `AbstractModel` cost hook defaults `DB289946DCE7F74D`, `CardModel.EnergyCost` `D6AF1AAE1A2FEE0D` and `DynamicVars` `19C079D25E68C079`, `DynamicVarSet.Keys` `E7FAD3B477754438`. Another mod's patch on any of them (and on `StableShuffle<CardModel>`) leaves the sort to the game. An exception faults the part (the game's comparison runs, its own exception again). One answer in 1,024 (Debug 16, `bdperf checks off` 1,024) is compared with the game's `CompareTo`: a difference faults it. Inside `ListenerCache.RunVanilla` no scope opens.
- **Setting**: a part of `pile_lookup` (card pile lookup: it already speaks of reshuffles; the option is unavailable only when neither PileIndex nor this applied). Its description and its 13 translations unchanged.
- **Proof hooks**: effects `CardSortKeys` (sorts answered) and `CardSortKeys.Answers` (comparisons), listed only where it applies; `counters` prints sorts, answers, refusals with the last reason, checks, mismatches; `bdperf sortkeys`: the local draw, discard and hand cards sorted with the whole mod off and with the part (the piles' order and two shuffled ones), then `StableShuffle` both ways from one seed: same order element by element, times both ways; `nothing to compare` when not applied or refused; restores the effect counters (only the game's sorts count). `bdperf sortkeys wrong`: answers reversed (positive control `sortkeys-wrong`, v0.113+ only).
- **Proof** (v0.113.1, Debug): `bdperf sortkeys` 959 cards, 87 kinds: the game's sort 1,245 ms, with the part 6.7 ms, identical orders (30,387 answers, 4,549 checked against the game's); `effects-check` 17 sorts, 153,177 answers, 11,480 checked, 0 mismatch, 0 refusal; `ab-2k` 54, `shuffle` 55, `fool-2k` 153, `combats3-2k` 63, `pile-cards-2k` 131, `clone` 4, `deckcount` 131 lines identical; every patched method rebuilt: `ab-2k` identical; co-op `effects-coop` and `coopfast` on/off identical per peer (58 lines), `coopclone`, `audit-coopdeath`, `coop4` agree; `coop4leak` (4 players, 20 run states) agrees with no disconnect (before: a client timed out at a combat start, its `AfterRoomEntered` frame 6.4 to 6.7 s, now 0.85 to 1.56 s); sweeps relics 4,740, cards 4,269, powers 6,139, encounters 3,321, potions 348 lines identical; `sortkeys-wrong` caught (`bdperf sortkeys` MISMATCH, the part off after its own check); mutations 40/40; on v0.107.1 and v0.111.0 "not needed", `ab-2k` identical, effects unchanged. A combat start at ~1,900 cards, nothing else running: `AfterRoomEntered` 0.42 s (0.92 s before; v0.107.1 0.10 s), longest frame 0.65 s (v0.107.1 0.52 s).

### Audit of 2026-11-13: BaseLib 3.4.8, Limitless 1.2.1, beta v0.113, every patch

Five read-only audits (listener caches; snapshot and relic reuse; hook filtering; Limitless and BaseLib patches with the settings; infrastructure and UI), then three reviews of the resulting diff. Each finding checked against the decompiled code before a change. Fixed:

- **Dead back-offs** (since they were written): five "another mod patches what this reproduces" checks started from `long.MinValue`, so `now - at >= 2000` overflowed and never ran: LimitlessReserialize, LimitlessHookSkip, three in SnapshotCardReuse (pile and card serialization, upgrade bits). Started at `long.MinValue / 2`; they don't fire in the reference setup (only BaseLib's allowed postfixes there).
- **BaseLib SavedSpireFields on cards or enchantments** (a content mod's per-card field): their value is exported into the saved properties by BaseLib's `FromInternal` postfix, which the property plans, the per-card snapshot memo and `SavesNoProperty` didn't see (stale saved state, a checksum difference). `BaseLibTypes.SpireFieldTargets` (BaseLib's registry, read again when it grows) leaves such types without a shortcut; the stamps include the registry's size; `FromInternal` patches other than BaseLib's (fingerprinted) count as a conflict.
- **What a reuse skips calling** must back off when another mod patches it (the rule): SnapshotCardReuse's memo, trusted and moved piles (ToSerializable, SavedProperties, keyword getters); CardDbScan (`IdCardIfNecessary`, Limitless's ledger allowed); HookSkip and HookFilter (`Hook.IterateCombatHookListeners`, now fingerprinted for HookFilter too, with v0.113's `ShouldSkipCombatEffect` it calls, and the getters its ending check reads; HookSkip's choice-context helpers, checked once a second); DeckCountMemo (the counted getters, the count lambda); LimitlessCloneMemo (the whole clone chain: FromSerializable, saved properties, enchantment, upgrades, MutableClone, dynamic variables, CardCmd.Enchant, the card and enchantment types' overrides on the way; BaseLib's and Limitless's patches there allowed by fingerprint, Limitless's uncapped options in the stamp); PileIndex (`Player.Piles`; every add, remove and clear checked by its effect when another mod patches one; an add or remove another mod's prefix skipped, or did itself, judged by the pile's count: only for a prefix registered after PileIndex's, see "Audit of 2026-11-15"); HoverTipReuse, RelicFlashMerge, CardFlightLimit (the effects, sounds and souls they skip; SpeedX's soul postfix allowed); IdleButtonInput (`_Input`, the drag check).
- **LimitlessHookSkip turned on during a combat** dropped stacked enchantments its walks had never noted (one that reacts, e.g. SlumberingEssence on BeforeFlush: a desync): it now arms outside a combat only (the next combat's piles are noted as they fill), and needs LimitlessReserialize applied.
- **PileIndex**: a move whose event handler throws after the list changed was undone in the index (the card answered no pile); now kept when the pile changed by that card, `Fail` when it changed otherwise. Not applied when a run already exists (its cards wouldn't be indexed). Reorders always checked by their count.
- **CardFlightLimit**: a flush stands for several cards only when the pile's only listeners are pile buttons' own `AddCard` (not overridden, not patched); otherwise one event per card. Re-entrant flushes queue for the next one.
- **IdleButtonInput**: turned off (`bdopt`, a fault, another mod's patch), buttons it had muted got their input back only when pressed (`bdopt off` wasn't the unmodified game for idle buttons); now within 30 frames (about half a second at 60 fps).
- **Smaller**: PileCounterBatch's clear depth paired through `__state`; GridSortCache judges each comparer method, not each sort order; LimitlessRecalcSkip's quiet passes see BaseLib registrations; the snapshot verifier also runs while the listener cache backs off; FastListeners and BaseLibTypes find model and BaseLib-derived types through other mods' assemblies (transitive references), not by assembly name; the left run's listener lists released at `RunManager.CleanUp` (or at the end of the scope it ran in); ListenerCache invalidates on v0.113's dominion setter; `PatchSafety`'s allowed-patch cache keyed by the allowed set; the load check accepts a protected member only from a derived class (an update making a used member protected is now a clean skip).

Not changed, on purpose: the fingerprint algorithm doesn't hash a method's own signature, callees' return types or field types (a change there nearly always changes the IL that uses them; changing the algorithm would change every constant of three versions for no case seen); a virtual a patch prefixes could get an override in a subclass (CardFlightLimit, the only one relying on it, now checks the actual handler).

Proof (Debug, BaseLib 3.4.8, Limitless 1.2.1, the final DLL, no baselines; three full batteries, the last one after the last review's fixes):

### Co-op with a player without the mod (2026-10-24, verified)

Local co-op, ~3,750 cards per player, Workshop mods, the client's mod off (`bdopt off`) as the stand-in for a player without it:
- `coopvanilla` (both play at once): divergence at the first action, with v0.4.0 and with v0.3.0 alike. The state dumps (the game logs both sides' states on a divergence) show the actions ran in a different order.
- `coopsolo1` (only the modded host plays): divergence. The unmodded client took 33 s to start the combat; the host's first plays reached it meanwhile and were dropped (`ActionQueueSet`: "currently cancelling all combat actions"; Limitless logs these too: "combat action(s) were rejected since the last combat start (healthy sessions show 0 or 1)").
- `coopsolo2` (only the modded client plays): no divergence but a frozen run: the unmodded host dropped the client's plays and its end of turn, so the enemy turn never came.
- `coop` (both modded) and `coopoff` (both off): no divergence.

Cause: the game's own handling of actions received during a combat start, made near certain by a speed gap of tens of seconds (combat start ~4 s with the mod, 30+ s without; the faster combat start option alone skips ~70 s of waits). Hence `affects_gameplay: true` and `FastCombatStart` always on in co-op (a player with that option off would start ~70 s late). Verified: `coop-required.ps1` (client with this mod disabled) gets the game's "Mods mismatch: the host has mods we don't have: BigDeckOptimization-v0.4.0" and can't join; `coopfast` (both modded, the client's fast start off) skips the waits on both sides, identical states, no rejected action. Two modded players on very different PCs can still meet it, as in vanilla, with a much smaller window.

### Co-op desyncs (earlier hypothesis)

The group often desynced. `RecalculateCardValues` only runs for the local player, and Limitless' `MultiEnchantment.RecalculateValues` rewrites its serialized nested cards (`SetSerializableCards`), which are part of the checksum. SpeedX's own log also warns its multiplayer timer override risks desyncs. Closed: no desynced session's logs ever came, and the desyncs reproduced since were the speed gap of players with and without the mod ("Co-op with a player without the mod" above, Step J), hence `affects_gameplay: true`; Limitless's re-serialization stays the game's own work, only skipped when it would give the same checksummed content (`LimitlessReserialize`).

## Implemented patches (`src/BigDeckOptimization/Patches`)

Safety nets around every patch (v0.6.1, after v0.6.0 broke combat on game beta v0.111.0, where `AbstractModel.ModifyDamageCap` gained a parameter: HookFilter's prefix couldn't be JIT-compiled, so it threw on its first call before its `Enabled` check and try/catch, and every damage calculation threw even with the mod switched off):
